# A full-depth root constraint on the one-auxiliary absorption distribution

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Main-agent manual reasoning after explicit human continuation, 2026-10-08. No mathematical program or additional model call. This extends the scoped H3 absorption argument in projection_flow_search_20261008_r2/REPORT.md; it does not give a GD lower bound or a full-W upper.

## Interface and scope

Use exactly the sufficient original-normal PSD certificate class of that report: all physical gradients, one auxiliary p=P_D(x0/M), and zero; M=1+2S. Its full cancellation/contact argument forces auxiliary absorption weights w_i>=0 with sumw_i=2. Setting z=sumw_i g_i/2 yields

    R_cert=M||p-z||^2+J_z+sum_(physical i,j)mu_ij Q_ij,
    J_z=2<x0,z>-M||z||^2-U1(x_n).

The physical Q are <=0 on actual histories. Since a globally PSD polynomial can be tested at the FORMAL p=z, every such certificate necessarily has J_z>=0 on every actual physical history. No conic-duality converse is assumed. The source interface remains an unaccepted joint derivation.

## Full actual word and an explicit legal physical history

Let m>=1536, eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, Lambda=1+sqrt2,
c=Lambda(1+eps),a=beta/c,b=c-2,u=Lambda-2. Use the actual same-m word
H_m=(H_j,1+d,H_j),j=m-1,d=a*c^j.
Put C=1+sumH_j, r=d/C,

    S=2C+d-1=C(2+r)-1, M=1+2S, C_parent=1+S.

The body D=[-r,1] and original start x0=C give gradients1 throughout the left H_j and at the central query. The central update lands at-d. Every right prefix with mass T<=C-1 has position-d+rT<=-r. Thus its gradient is-r, and the actual terminal is x_n=g_n=-r. These bounds cover every physical step and use one fixed interval body.

Let alpha=(sum of w_i at left H_j and central-query indices)/2. Then z=(1+r)alpha-r, U1(x_n)=r^2. Consequently every certificate in the stated class must obey

    2Cz-Mz^2-r^2>=0.                              (1)

The canonical distribution w_i=2h_i/M for i<n, w_n=2(1+S)/M has

    alpha0=(C+d)/M, z0=(C-Sr)/M.

Its primitive energy is

    V=C^2-C_parent^2 r^2
     =-C^2(r^2+2r-1)(1+r)^2.

With t=z-z0=(1+r)(alpha-alpha0), equation(1) becomes

    0<=V/M+2Sr*t-Mt^2.                            (2)

When r>u, V<0. Thus t>0 and dropping only the nonpositive square gives

    alpha-alpha0 >=
      C^2(r^2+2r-1)(1+r)/(2MSr).                 (3)

In particular canonical absorption is impossible regardless of all physical row weights or topology.

## A uniform explicit transfer lower bound

The exact word mass is

    C=(a/b)c^j+(1-a/b)2^j,
    r=b/[1+kappa(2/c)^j], kappa=b/a-1=(cb-beta)/beta.

The elementary same-m parameter bounds are
12/5<Lambda, 2<c<17/7, 0<b<3/7, and eps<1/10.
Since cb=1+(Lambda^2+1)eps+Lambda^2 eps^2 and Lambda<5/2,
cb<1+8eps in this range. Hence0<kappa<10eps.
Also2/c<5/6 and j>=16. The rational inequality(5/6)^8<1/4 gives
kappa(2/c)^j<5eps/8. Therefore

    r>b(1-5eps/8)
      =u+eps(Lambda-5b/8)
      >u+eps(12/5-15/56)>u+2eps.

Thus r^2+2r-1=(r-u)(r+Lambda)>(28/5)eps.
Using S<C(2+r),M<2C(2+r),r<1/2 and2+r<5/2 in(3) gives

    alpha-alpha0 >
      (r^2+2r-1)(1+r)/[4r(2+r)^2]
      > (56/125)eps > eps/4 = 1/(16m).           (4)

This is a necessary absorption transfer at the root of the FULL H_m, uniformly for every original m>=1536. It is not merely a fixed seven-step obstruction. Different roots/subtrees cannot be summed without proving they constrain the SAME global certificate and SAME applicable histories.

## Interpretation and boundary

The legal interval history has normalized actual objective r^2/C^2; its role here is to test the stronger fixed-mixture certificate requirement, not to defeat17/(16D_m). Equation(4) does not rule out a different absorption distribution, additional auxiliary points, a direct radius certificate, or the desired full-W upper.

The missing item remains a quantitative all-depth PSD estimate after an admissible redistribution. This note supplies no such estimate. No formal submission, fact promotion, numerical replay, or new resource grant.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000673 -->
# A whole-class obstruction for a fixed terminal fan

SELF-AUTHORED analytical draft, JOINT UNVERIFIED. The active native boundary-transport call independently checked the main aggregate-cut argument before writing its report. No mathematical program or formal submission was used. The final rerouting corollary below is a parent derivation, not a separately verified fact. Original target OPEN.

## 1. Scope and fixed source

Use the actual same-enclosing-m prefix P_m of H_m, m>=1536, with only its final update b=1+a removed. All query labels0,...,l are retained. The fixed parameters are epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a=beta/c. Let D=1+2sum H_m and q=17/(16D).

Use the source profile from prefix_source_profile_search_20261008_r3/REPORT.md:

    alpha=1/(2c), gamma=1/sqrt(c), zeta=1-alpha-gamma,
    u^(j)=H_j/S_j, pi^(1)=(1),
    pi^(j)=(alpha u^(j-1),zeta,gamma pi^(j-1)), mu=2pi^(m), nu=0.

All these choices remain restrictions on this construction, not necessities for the original target. At the full root write k=2^(m-1)-1, Lblock={0,...,k-1}, H=S_(m-1), R=1+a c^(m-1), z=1_Lblock, and t_i=1+sum_(j<i)h_j. Then

    A=sum_(i<k)mu_i=1/c, u=mu_k=2zeta,
    mu_i=A h_i/H for i<k, D=1+4H+2R.

For each exact original multiplier array define N_ij=-lambda_ij off diagonal, N_ii=mu_i+sum_(j!=i)lambda_ij, L_ii=1,L_ij=h_j for j<i, B=sym(NL), and K=B-a^2 e_l e_l^T-mu mu^T/(4q). These use the full original source/radius polynomial; no sharp-contact kernel or diagonal-residual restriction is imposed.

The class to exclude is EVERY array

    lambda_ij=1_(i=l)mu_j+C_ij, i!=j,

where C_ij>=0 has zero query divergence at every label. Source rows remain lambda_(i,star)=mu_i, lambda_(star,i)=0. There is no restriction on support, cycle length, rank, intensities, or how C was found. In particular both symmetric and oriented dyadic cycles and all changes to their landing probabilities belong to this class. The fixed terminal fan is the load-bearing hypothesis.

## 2. The aggregate identity

Let B_C be the B contribution of C alone. Exact zero divergence gives

    z^T B_C z = -sum_(j outside Lblock,i in Lblock) C_ji (H-t_i).

One way to derive it is to set every formal gradient on Lblock equal to a common vector and every other gradient to zero. Lg has coefficient t_i inside Lblock and H outside it. The internal directed terms cancel by the zero-divergence identity, leaving precisely the displayed boundary sum.

At the root bridge,

    (B_C)_kk=sum_(i<k)C_ki-(R-1)sum_(j>k)C_kj.

The actual final step within H_(m-1) is b, so H-t_i>=a for every i<k. Therefore

    z^T B_C z+a(B_C)_kk <=0.

The base terminal fan has z^T B0 z=sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i and (B0)_kk=u. Consequently every matrix in the class satisfies

    z^T K z+a K_kk
    <=sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i+a u-(A^2+a u^2)/(4q).

There is no terminal-gradient penalty in these two tests because neither z nor e_k includes the final query l.

## 3. Strict all-depth negative bound

The elementary same-m bounds are12/5<c<5/2,2/5<a<1/2,7/5<b<3/2,H>4096. Moreover R/H>a>2/5: writing v=a/(c-2), one has0<v<1 and 1+S_j=(1-v)2^j+v c^j<c^j for j>0, while R>ac^(m-1). Thus D>24H/5.

Also gamma<13/20 because c>12/5>400/169, and alpha<5/24. Hence zeta>17/120 and u>17/60>7/25; the simple upper bounds give u<2/5. Since A>2/5,

    (A^2+a u^2)/(4q)
    >(96/85)H[4/25+98/3125]
    >27H/125.

On the other hand, exact summation of the original clocks gives

    sum_(i<k)h_i t_i=H+(H^2-sum_(i<k)h_i^2)/2.

Using A<5/12 and a u<1/5 therefore yields

    sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i+a u <5H/24+37/60.

Subtracting,

    z^T K z+a K_kk
    <37/60-23H/3000
    <-H/150<0,

where the last step follows from H>4096>1850/3. At least one of these two quadratic tests is strictly negative. The complete original matrix cannot be PSD. This closes the entire stated fixed-terminal-fan plus nonnegative-circulation class, not just the two clock-weighted candidate laws.

This is a formal coefficient-class obstruction. The test assignments need not obey the original interpolation inequalities and are not legal GD counterexamples.

## 4. Quantitative necessary reselection of the terminal fan

Now allow arbitrary nonnegative query multipliers with the SAME source mu and exact value flow, without requiring lambda>=lambda0. Their incoming-star coefficients must still sum to zero because sum mu=2; hence nu=0.

The full initial-block/root cut from the predecessor is

    HA+a u-[z^T B z+a B_kk]
    =sum_(j>k,i<k)(H-t_i)lambda_ji
     +sum_(i<k)(H-t_i-a)lambda_ki
     +a(R-1)sum_(j>k)lambda_kj.

Every displayed coefficient is nonnegative. Isolate the terminal rows lambda_(l,i), then compare with the base fan. This gives

    z^T B z+a B_kk
    <=sum_(i<k)mu_i t_i+a u
      +sum_(i<k)(H-t_i)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+.

If the general certificate is PSD, Section3 therefore forces

    sum_(i<k)(H-t_i)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+ >H/150,

and, because H-t_i<=H,

    sum_(i<k)(mu_i-lambda_(l,i))_+ >1/150.

Thus a successful certificate with this source must actually lower some of the original terminal-to-left coefficients by a quantitatively nonzero amount. Adding more nonnegative cycles cannot do that. This does NOT license negative final multipliers: every final original lambda must remain nonnegative and exact value flow must be rebuilt.

A concrete unallocated next idea is to reroute some terminal-to-left source mass through later left queries before it reaches its final source, replacing the base fan itself. That changes the weighted incoming-clock cost in the displayed identity. No compatible full PSD construction, amount-allocation rule or all-depth upper proof is supplied here; merely naming such a relay is not progress toward the theorem without its full coefficients and matrix bound.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000674 -->
# Boundary-suppressed return transport: an unallocated analytical lead

SELF-AUTHORED, JOINT UNVERIFIED. No mathematical program, verification or new native allocation is implied. Original target OPEN. The completed source-profile call must be read in full before turning this lead into a successor task.

## An exact necessary first-query capacity

Use the full weighted-prefix formulation with first physical step h0=1+a, 0<a<1, more than one query, q>0, nonnegative original multipliers, and exact value flow. Let mu_i=lambda_(i,star), nu_i=lambda_(star,i); let out_i,in_i count only other physical query labels. With N_ii=mu_i+out_i, N_ij=-lambda_ij and B=sym(NL), the first diagonal is

    B_00=mu_0+(1-h0)out_0=mu_0-a out_0.

The full Schur condition therefore forces

    0<=mu_0<=4q,
    out_0<=[mu_0-mu_0^2/(4q)]/a<=q/a.

Value flow at the nonterminal label0 gives in_0=mu_0+out_0-nu_0. Maximizing the right side over0<=mu_0<=4q gives the additional bound

    in_0<=q(1+a)^2/a.

No contact assumption, nu=0 premise, sparsity, or diagonal-residual restriction was used. These are necessary first-query bounds, not sufficient certificates or a whole-source exclusion. All notation and algebra should be independently checked before use.

## Why a different landing measure is genuinely different

The current native source-profile report says its oriented cycle law sends the root's return traffic into each left query in proportion to h_i/H. The root requires traffic of order D while this rule sends a fraction of order1/D to query0. That creates order-one outgoing traffic at a query whose capacity is order1/D. Changing only cycle intensity cannot fix that mismatch. This paragraph is a scope summary of the native report, not a new general theorem.

For a left block of nonnegative step masses k_i and total H, define its local clock t_i=1+sum_(j<i)k_j. A different explicit landing distribution is proportional to k_i t_i. Its normalization is exactly

    Z1=sum k_i t_i=H+(H^2-sum k_i^2)/2.

If max k_i<=theta H with theta<1, then Z1>=H+(1-theta)H^2/2. Thus its first weight can be of order1/H^2 instead of1/H. Traffic of order H then produces order1/H at that boundary, which removes the preceding order mismatch. All constants and traffic bounds still need proof on the actual same-m hierarchy.

A further danger is accumulation over many ancestors. In a hypothetical law with node traffic of order H_v^2/D, a linear-clock first weight of order H_v^-2 contributes order1/D from each ancestor; this alone does not give a uniform first-query bound. A quadratic-clock distribution proportional to k_i t_i^2 could change that order, but no corresponding normalization bound, complete coefficients, global flow or PSD proof is asserted here. This is a conditional scaling diagnostic, not an exclusion of every linear-clock law or proof of the quadratic rule.

## Required reopening object

A useful successor must specify the original cycle coefficients at every node, their actual node traffic, both destination distributions, unchanged source/value flow, all boundary leaves and the full non-diagonal Schur matrix. It must check the summed first-query traffic as well as other query diagonals and mixed blocks. Passing an order estimate or the old root cut is insufficient. Every formal negative direction must be distinguished from a legal GD function. The operator's original full target remains open.

# Exact radial square reserve for two actual resolvents

SELF-AUTHORED analytical lead, JOINT UNVERIFIED. No mathematical program or formal submission. This note supplies a local reserve only, not an all-depth payment or an upper bound for the original problem.

Let D be one fixed compact convex set containing zero. Fix z, d>1 and s>=0, and put g=P_D(z), q=P_D(z/d), delta=g-q. Both projections are actual points of the same body. Their two original projection normals give

N1=<z-g,g-q>>=0,
N2=<z-dq,q-g>>=0.

Direct expansion gives N1+N2=(d-1)<q,delta>-||delta||^2. Therefore

<q,delta>=[||delta||^2+N1+N2]/(d-1).

For the radial term already present in the accepted finite-block identity,

Lrad(s,z,d)=2s<z,g-q>+s^2(||g||^2-||q||^2),

substituting <z,delta>=||delta||^2+<q,delta>+N1 yields the exact identity

Lrad(s,z,d)
= [2sd+s^2(d+1)]/(d-1) ||g-q||^2
  + [2s(d+s)]/(d-1) N1
  + [2s(1+s)]/(d-1) N2.

Every coefficient is nonnegative. In the original augmented-row orientation Q_ij=<X_j-G_j,G_i-G_j><=0, N1=-Q_(q,g) and N2=-d Q_(g,q), where the auxiliary input for q is z/d. Thus the identity does not import a new legality assumption or replace the physical trajectory. If d=1 then q=g and Lrad=0; the singular displayed coefficients are not used at that endpoint.

A possible use is to pay differences along a growing tree of actual radial auxiliary edges, in combination with bridge squares and full original remainders. Such a tree payment would still need its exact inequality, original-row coefficients, parameter dependence and target constant. Nothing here proves that it exists or that the earlier bounded-feature obstructions are bypassed.

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000675 -->

# Unallocated lead: aim directly at relaxed U rather than exact U

UNREVIEWED MAIN ANALYTICAL LEAD, no new grant, program or independent audit.

The original sufficient target is W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m), whereas the current flow discovery tests the stronger exact U1(T_hx)<=U_M(x),M=1+2sumh.
For alpha=17/16 it would suffice to establish

    U1(T_hx)<=alpha U_M(x)

for every compact convex D containing0 and all starts. Indeed U_M(x)<=dist(x,argminF_D)^2/M, by pairing each g inD with the closest polar minimum and completing a square. Accepted finite-word projection-simplex universality would then give the required full-function bound. No relaxed inequality is established here.

A certificate for J_alpha=alpha U_M(x0)-U1(xn) changes the coefficient interface. Star rows can no longer be dropped. Keep original Q_ij=<X_j-G_j,G_i-G_j><=0, G_star=X_star=0, scale mu_ij=a_j lambda_ij for nonstar destinations, and permit star->j as well as p->physical edges.
Exact x0 cancellation requires

    div_i(mu)=2(1_i=n-alpha*1_i=p) for nonstar i,
    div_star(mu)=2(alpha-1),

so total star-source mass is2(alpha-1)=1/8. Incoming-to-star rows are identically zero and may be assigned0. For alpha=1 these equations reduce to the old forced-zero star source.

The value-free gradient polynomial is

    R_alpha =
      2<g_n,sumh_i g_i>+||g_n||^2-alpha M||p||^2
      +sum_(nonstar i,j)mu_ij<B_jG,G_j-G_i>
      +sum_(j nonstar)mu_(star,j)<B_jG,G_j>.

At common gradients G_i=v for all nonstar nodes,

    R_alpha(v)=[(1-alpha)M+sum_j mu_(star,j)t_j]||v||^2.

This need not be zero. Consequently old common-gradient clock-contact equations, p absorption, and quotientg_n=0 cannot be imposed as necessary conditions. The full gradient matrix must be retained unless a new exact reduction is proved.

Any later task must independently derive this interface, translate the actual full-W threshold, and construct a quantitative whole-hierarchy certificate. Relabeling the new PSD problem is not progress. The previous exact-U tree and canonical-absorption obstructions do not automatically extend to this relaxed star-source class.

# Necessary backward flow and an all-depth free-absorption tree obstruction

JOINT UNVERIFIED. Main-agent manual derivation after explicit continuation. No program, formal submission or extra model. Uses the jointly unverified original-normal interface in projection_flow_search_20261008_r2/REPORT.md, re-expressed below. No full-function GD counterexample is asserted.

## 1. General original-normal conditions

For the same body, gradients and one auxiliary p, any considered full PSD certificate has nonnegative mu, divergence2(e_n-e_p), clock drift2h_i at physical i<n and0 atn,p. Set S=sumh,M=1+2S,t_i=1+sum_(k<i)h_k,t_p=M, and w_i=mu_(i,p). The auxiliary is absorbing and sumw_i=2.

The formal gradient quadratic is

    R=2<g_n,sumh_i g_i>+||g_n||^2-M||p||^2
      +sum_ij mu_ij<B_jG,G_j-G_i>,
    B_jG=g_j+sum_(k<j)h_k g_k, B_pG=M p.

At a physical node i<n, define forward mass a_i=sum_(j>i,j<=n)mu_ij and backward mass b_i=sum_(j<i)mu_ij. Its diagonal coefficient is

    c_ii=w_i+b_i-(h_i-1)a_i.                       (1)

This follows from incoming physical rows contributing in_i, each forward outgoing row contributing -h_i mu_ij, and full divergence at i equal zero. The p diagonal is M and the p--i matrix entry is -Mw_i/2. Testing only g_i and p therefore forces

    c_ii>=Mw_i^2/4>=0.                            (2)

These are necessary full-space PSD conditions, not inequalities assumed only on legal histories.

## 2. Root backward-flow requirement for the actual H_m

Use m>=1536 and the original same-m parameters. At central physical index i=2^(m-1)-1 put

    d=a*c^(m-1), h_i=1+d,
    C=1+sumH_(m-1), S=2C+d-1, M=4C+2d-1.

The earlier mass formula gives C<c^(m-1), hence d/C>a>2/5. Thus

    C<(5/2)d, C+d<(7/2)d, M<12d.

Write a_i=A,b_i=B,w_i=w. From sumw=2, w<=2. Equations(1)-(2) imply

    d A<=B+2, w^2<=4(B+2)/M.                     (3)

The clock drift at this physical root equals2(1+d). Every forward physical clock difference is at most t_n-t_i=C+d. Dropping only the nonpositive backward terms gives

    2(1+d) <= A(C+d)+w(M-C)
             <= (7/2)(B+2)+2 sqrt(M(B+2))
             < (7/2)(B+2)+2 sqrt(12d(B+2)).       (4)

If B+2<=d/16, the final right side divided by d is less than

    7/32+sqrt3 <7/32+7/4=63/32<2,

contradicting2(1+d)>2d. Consequently EVERY certificate in this full single-auxiliary original-normal PSD class requires

    sum_(j<i)mu_(i,j) > d/16-2.                  (5)

This allows arbitrary physical topology and all absorption weights. It does not forbid large, nonlocal or cyclic flows; it quantitatively requires them at the central large step.

## 3. Exact parameterization on an increasing physical tree

Consider any physical tree rooted at n, with parent pi(v)>v, both edge directions, and all absorption edges. For a non-root subtree rooted at v, let A_v be its total PHYSICAL step mass and W_v its total absorption mass. Let L_v=t_parent-t_v>0.

Summing physical divergences over the subtree gives

    mu_(parent,v)=mu_(v,parent)+W_v.

Summing clock drift over the same subtree, and telescoping every internal edge, gives

    mu_(v,parent)L_v+W_v(M-t_v)=2A_v.

The telescoping identity used here is
sum_(internal child edges u) W_u(t_parent(u)-t_u)
 =sum_(subtree nodes u)w_u(t_v-t_u).
Thus both edge weights are uniquely determined:

    mu_(v,parent)=[2A_v-W_v(M-t_v)]/L_v,
    mu_(parent,v)=[2A_v-W_v(M-t_parent)]/L_v.       (6)

This does not prescribe absorption or canonical weights.

On the original binary tree pi(v)=v+2^nu2(v+1), the subtree consists of the complete left H_l and its level-l bridge. Its mass equals the clock gap:

    A_v=S_l+1+a*c^l=L_v.

Since W_v>=0 and M>t_parent, equation(6) gives the universal directed downward bound

    0<=mu_(parent,v)<=2.                          (7)

The full-word central root i has exactly m-1 lower-index children. Therefore its backward mass on this tree is at most2(m-1).

## 4. Whole free-absorption tree family is excluded

For m>=1536, a>2/5 and c>2 give d>(2/5)2^(m-1)>100m. The last inequality holds at m=16 and is preserved under doubling; our range is larger.
Equation(5) then requires

    B>d/16-2>(25/4)m-2>2(m-1),

contradicting(7).

Thus no PSD certificate in this specified bidirectional physical binary-tree topology exists on the actual H_m, m>=1536, even when every absorption weight is free. This is stronger than the earlier canonical-absorption exclusion.

The result does not classify H3 or H4, does not prohibit the complete physical graph, and does not prove an actual violation of the U inequality or of17/(16D_m). A future graph must accommodate the growing backward mass(5); simply retuning absorption on this tree cannot suffice. All statements remain a joint unverified analytical draft.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000678 -->
# Huber contact for the weighted-prefix objective

SELF-AUTHORED analytical lead; JOINT UNVERIFIED. No mathematical program. Original target OPEN. This note does not identify any numerical PEP optimum as exact.

Fix a nonnegative prefix k=(k_0,...,k_(p-1)), total s, and 0<=a<1. The weighted terminal criterion is V=2f_p-(1-a^2)||g_p||^2 under original radius one and advertised smoothness one. Put K=2+2s-a^2>0. If this prefix is obtained by deleting the last step b=1+a from a full word of total mass S, then K=1+2S-b^2.

## A universal exact lower witness for this criterion

Take the one-dimensional Huber function F_t(x)=x^2/2 for |x|<=t and t|x|-t^2/2 otherwise, with t=1/K and x0=1. Its clipped gradient is continuous, nondecreasing and 1-Lipschitz; zero is its unique minimizer. Every actual prefix point is x_i=1-t sum_(j<i) k_j. Since K-(1+s)=1+s-a^2>0, x_i>t for all i. Thus every queried gradient equals t and

V=2t-(2+2s-a^2)t^2=1/K.

Consequently the exact full PEP value is at least 1/K for EVERY nonnegative prefix. If the proposed sufficient target V<=17/(16D), D=1+2S, is to hold, then necessarily D>=17b^2. This is not an upper bound or a full-word lower counterexample. Smoothness only gives 2f_after<=V.

## Contact equations for a hypothetical sharp dual

Retain all original rows Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0, including both minimizer directions and incoming final-query rows. Let lambda>=0 be their multipliers and let q be the coefficient on the original squared radius. Write mu_i=lambda_(i,star), nu_i=lambda_(star,i). Exact value cancellation gives

sum_i mu_i=2+sum_i nu_i.

Let L be lower triangular, L_ii=1 and L_ij=k_j for j<i. Set N_ij=-lambda_ij off diagonal and N_ii=mu_i+sum_(j!=i)lambda_ij. Then N e=mu and N^T e=2 e_p+nu. With B=sym(NL), the full quadratic after value cancellation is

P=q||x0||^2-<x0,sum_i mu_i g_i>+sum_(i,j)B_ij<g_i,g_j>-a^2||g_p||^2.

For a genuine sharp dual at q=1/K, Huber attainment makes P vanish on x0=K v, g_i=v. All actual Q_i,star and chronological Q_ij vanish there, whereas Q_star,i=-t x_i+t^2<0. Since the dual is PSD and all multipliers nonnegative, this forces every nu_i=0, hence sum mu_i=2. Equivalently, the common Huber direction must be a null vector of the PSD matrix, which yields

N tclock+2 L^T e_p-2a^2 e_p=K mu, where tclock=L e.

Pre-freeze correction, October8: the first draft incorrectly replaced the transposed term L^T N^T e by L^T mu. The correct identity uses N^T e=2e_p when nu=0. The source-profile agent found this before any extraction; the original bytes are preserved in main_weighted_prefix_huber_contact_20261008_r3_PRE_CORRECTION.md. The displayed Huber witness and threshold necessity are unaffected. This correction remains an independently noticed draft correction, not formal verification.

These are exact contact restrictions at q=1/K ONLY. At the desired relaxed q=17/(16D), Huber contact need not be tight, nu may be nonzero, and none of these additional null identities may be imposed as necessary. A law deliberately choosing nu=0 is still a legitimate restricted construction, but its failure does not refute the full target.

## What remains

The finite calibrated P3/P4/P5 solves suggest inspecting the freely optimized source profile, but this note makes no numerical equality claim and does not reload or analyze saved arrays. The two previous source laws were proportional to the raw clock, or changed only the first and final source entries; both fail at full enclosing depth. A source profile changing across the dyadic hierarchy is a new possible mechanism only if paired with a complete nonnegative original-row construction and a full PSD proof. Necessary source/cut inequalities alone do not provide such a certificate.

<!-- END_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000678 -->

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000681 -->
# A growing shifted-circle chain: actual coupled dynamics and the unpaid input flux

Status: JOINT UNVERIFIED ANALYTICAL DRAFT. The growing policy passes the
scale screen. Its complete retained terminal payoff is NOT bounded sharply
enough here to prove either a benchmark witness or a complete obstruction.
A specific nonnegative input flux remains unpaid. No formal gate was
allocated; no assertion below has been promoted to a fact.

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926; worker max3; one round after baseline59,
completed ceiling60 / attempted ceiling61. TASK.md and guidance
7b221a98307db859 govern. Frozen-policy finding: eec80a72df26e757.
No construction repair was used. Previous drafts4c4a21a484564bdc and
ced9f5595b446c1d are context, not accepted premises. All ingredients below
are derived jointly. The only accepted mathematical input is the original
benchmark/ray conclusion of fact04219554e41d9750.

## 1. Frozen policy and the actual problem left open

For each integer m>=16 put

    lambda=1+sqrt(2), tau=1/4, k=floor(sqrt(m)), J=m-k,
    beta=1/k, eta_word=1-1/(4m), c=lambda*(1+1/(4m)),
    a_word=eta_word/c,
    H_0=(), H_(j+1)=(H_j,b_j,H_j), b_j=1+a_word*c^j,
    S_j=sum H_j, n_m=2^m-1.

All children retain the SAME enclosing m and eta_word,c,a_word. Circular
beta differs from eta_word. Select precisely the first occurrences of
levels J,J+1,...,m-1: k physical steps at zero-based positions
2^(J+i-1)-1 for i=1,...,k. No other step is changed or removed. The
unchanged word is exactly

    (H_J,b_J,H_J,b_(J+1),H_(J+1),b_(J+2),...,
                                      H_(m-2),b_(m-1),H_(m-1)).       (1.1)

Thus ordinary block masses are s_1=S_J, s_i=S_(J+i-2) for 2<=i<=k,
and s_(k+1)=S_(m-1). Selected step i is b_(J+i-1). These steps exceed2
for all sufficiently large m, the range used for all eventual conclusions.

The original benchmark, never recomputed here, is

    C_0=B_0=1,
    C_n=max_(0<=r<n) Omega(C_r,C_(n-r-1)),
    B_n=max_(0<=r<n) G(C_r,B_(n-r-1)),
    Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt((x+y)^2+4xy))/2,
    G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2, U_n^ZJ=1/B_n.

Every integer split r is included. Fact04219554e41d9750 gives

    0<Z_1=lim_m B_(2^m-1)/lambda^m<infinity.                       (1.2)

For the static convex1-smooth F below with minimum0, write
E_m=2F(x_(n_m))/dist(x_0,argmin F)^2. Multiplying F by any advertised
L>0 gives x^+=x-(h/L)grad(LF)=x-h grad F and the same payoff. This is
a particular lower witness for W_n(h), not an identity for its supremum.
The construction gives the EXACT decomposition

    B_(n_m) E_m = T_m+B_(n_m) V_m/R_m^2,                         (1.3)
    T_m -> T_infty:=Z_1*exp(-17/4)/(2lambda)>0.                   (1.4)

V_m retains ALL k circular values at the actual terminal iterate; R_m
is the true closest initial distance. Equation(1.4) is a scale screen,
not the requested retained-history result. The unpaid term is identified
in Sections7--10, including all ordinary steps and large overshoots.

## 2. Preliminary scale screen

Sections3--6 jointly prove, uniformly over these k blocks,

    C_beta=beta+sqrt(1+2beta),
    M_i=C_beta*s_i/2+O(k^2 exp(2k)), R_m^2->1,
    D_(k+1)->(lambda-2)exp(-2), sigma_1~2/(C_beta*S_J).           (2.1)

The overlap smoothness bound remains at most1 for every k, so dimension
does not dilute the normalization. The final Huber term consequently
has the positive benchmark-scale limit(1.4), with no loss exponential
in k. These statements are proved below, not assumed as premises.

The retained caps and radius alone also do not force a deficit. For the
SAME function, the comparison point z=(0,-1,0,...,0) has norm1 and

    Phi_1(z_1,z_2)=sigma_1-(1+beta^2)*sigma_1^2/2,

while every later circular component is zero. Therefore

    B_(n_m)*Phi_1(z_1,z_2)/R_m^2
                  ~2Z_1*exp(-1/4)*lambda^k ->infinity.          (2.2)

This is solely a cap/radius screen. The point z is NOT asserted to be
on the actual trajectory. It shows why a geometric cap bound cannot
reject this policy as trivially diluted. The actual terminal V_m still
has to be paid; neither(2.2) nor(1.4) is a retained-history solution.

## 3. Circular envelope and every local physical step

For 0<beta<=1/4 set

    p(q)=beta+sqrt((1+beta)^2-(q+beta)^2), 0<=q<=1,
    v(q)=(p(q),-q), K=conv({0} union {v(q):0<=q<=1}).

The disk of center(beta,beta), radius1+beta contains0 because
2beta^2<=(1+beta)^2. Its radial vector(p(q)-beta,-q-beta) supports
K at v(q). The endpoints are(C_beta,0),(beta,-1), and the horizontal
segment from0 to(C_beta,0) is in K. Hence

    0<=p<=C_beta, 0<=q<=1, p>=beta q,
    (p,0) in K whenever(p,-q) in K.                              (3.1)

For compact convex C containing0 define
E_C(r)=max_(g in C){<g,r>-||g||^2/2}. Completing the square gives
the unique maximizer Pi_C(r), the Euclidean projection. Its inequalities
at r,s give ||Pi_C(r)-Pi_C(s)||^2<=<Pi_C(r)-Pi_C(s),r-s>.
Comparing maximization formulas at r,r+d bounds the increment after
subtracting <Pi_C(r),d> by ||d||^2. Thus E_C is differentiable, convex,
nonnegative and1-smooth with gradient Pi_C. Its zero set is the polar
cone: a positive support value gives a positive objective at a small
positive multiple of the supporting vector. The cone of K is generated
by its endpoints, so

    E_(sigma K)(U,V)=0 iff U<=0 and beta U-V<=0.                  (3.2)

Define Hub_delta(t) to be0 for t<=0, t^2/2 for 0<=t<=delta, and
delta*t-delta^2/2 for t>=delta; its derivative is clip(t,0,delta).

Take an ordinary block a_1,...,a_N in its physical order, alpha_j=a_j/2.
Construct r_j=(u_j,-y_j) backwards from r_N=(beta,-1). For alpha_j>0
choose the unique q_(j-1) in[0,1) satisfying

    (q_(j-1)+beta)/(p(q_(j-1))-beta)
       =max{beta/(C_beta-beta),
                  (y_j+(1-alpha_j)beta)/(u_j-(1-alpha_j)beta)},
    r_(j-1)=r_j+alpha_j*v(q_(j-1)).                              (3.3)

For alpha_j=0 keep the state and its projection. The denominator is
positive because u_j>=beta and alpha_j>0; the left ratio increases
from beta/(C_beta-beta) to infinity. We have u_j>=beta,y_j>=1 and
q_(j-1)<=q_j: at an interior current point the ratio is
(y_j+beta)/(u_j-beta), and subtracting alpha_j beta from its numerator
while adding it to its denominator can only decrease it. At q_j=1
the upper ratio is infinite. At q_j=0 the normal-cone condition
y_j<=beta(u_j-C_beta)/(C_beta-beta) keeps the next point capped.

For q_(j-1)>0, equation(3.3) places r_(j-1)-v(q_(j-1)) on its
outward circular normal; y_(j-1)>=1>=q_(j-1) fixes the sign. For
q_(j-1)=0 it gives y_(j-1)<=beta(u_(j-1)-C_beta)/(C_beta-beta),
the endpoint cone generated by the circular normal and(0,1).
The criterion <r-g,w-g><=0 for every w in K proves

    Pi_K(r_j)=v(q_j),
    u_0=beta+sum alpha_j*p(q_(j-1)),
    y_0=1+sum alpha_j*q_(j-1), u_0>=beta*y_0.                    (3.4)

This is a symbolic finite definition, not an executed recurrence.
For incoming ell>=0,D>0 put

    M=u_0+y_0/beta, sigma=D/M, ell_out=sigma*y_0,
    A=ell+ell_out/beta,
    Phi(X,Y)=E_(sigma K)(X-A,Y-ell_out).                          (3.5)

The local states(A+sigma*u_j,ell_out-sigma*y_j) start at(ell+D,0)
and follow exactly -(a_j/2)grad Phi. Inputs exceed ell and outputs
are in[0,ell_out). The final gradient is sigma(beta,-1). A selected
b>2 has T=b/2-1,D_out=sigma*T and exact post-checkpoint state

    (X^+,Y^+)=(A-beta*D_out,ell_out+D_out).                      (3.6)

The component is zero at this instant by(3.2); later motion may
reactivate it. It is zero whenever X<=ell,Y>=0. Its output derivative
vanishes for EVERY X when Y>=ell_out: replacing(p,-q) by(p,0) in
its maximization increases the objective by q(Y-ell_out)+q^2/2
for q>0. This exact isolation pays the active-coordinate induction.

## 4. Static function, all global updates, smoothness and exact radius

Apply Section3 to every block in(1.1), obtaining u_i,y_i,M_i. Set
ell_1=0,D_1=1 and

    T_i=b_(J+i-1)/2-1, sigma_i=D_i/M_i,
    ell_(i+1)=sigma_i*y_i, A_i=ell_i+ell_(i+1)/beta,
    D_(i+1)=sigma_i*T_i,
    Phi_i(X,Y)=E_(sigma_i K)(X-A_i,Y-ell_(i+1)).

Fix, before GD, the function on R^(k+1)

    delta=D_(k+1)/(1+S_(m-1)),
    F(x)=1/2 sum_(i=1)^k Phi_i(x_i,x_(i+1))
                 +1/2 Hub_delta(x_(k+1)-ell_(k+1)), x_0=e_1.     (4.1)

The map stacking all coordinate pairs and the last scalar counts every
coordinate at most twice; its squared operator norm is at most2. Each
unscaled summand is convex1-smooth on its own coordinates. Thus the
factor1/2 makes F globally convex1-smooth. F>=0 and F(0)=0.

At ANY state define

    (P_i,-Q_i)=Pi_(sigma_i K)(x_i-A_i,x_(i+1)-ell_(i+1)),
    P_(k+1)=clip(x_(k+1)-ell_(k+1),0,delta), Q_0=0.

The full physical update, retaining all earlier components, is

    x_j^+=x_j-(h/2)(P_j-Q_(j-1)), 1<=j<=k+1.                   (4.2)

At the start of block i its input is ell_i+D_i and later coordinates
are zero. The local states have x_i>=ell_i, so Q_(i-1)=0 by isolation.
The next component has input at most ell_(i+1) and output0 and hence
vanishes, as do later components. Components before i-1 involve neither
active coordinate. Thus(4.2) realizes EVERY ordinary step in Section3
and its checkpoint(3.6). Earlier components still follow(4.2) and may
have nonzero values and gradients. They are never reset to zero.

During the final suffix, if s' is the mass already read, the last excess
is exactly D_(k+1)-s'*delta/2>=delta: the preceding output derivative
Q_k is zero in this region and the scalar derivative is delta, proving
the assertion by induction. Consequently

    2F(x_n)=sum_(i=1)^k Phi_i(x_(n,i),x_(n,i+1))
                       +D_(k+1)^2/[2(1+S_(m-1))].             (4.3)

From(3.2) and nonnegativity the ENTIRE minimum set is

    C_min={x: x_i<=A_i, x_(i+1)>=beta(x_i-ell_i), 1<=i<=k;
                                                x_(k+1)<=ell_(k+1)}.

The point x_*=(A_1,ell_2,0,...,0) belongs to it. Equations(3.4) give
1-A_1=sigma_1*u_1, ell_2=beta*A_1=sigma_1*y_1, and
A_1<=1/(1+beta^2). A feasible point with x_1<0 has squared distance
at least1 from e_1. For 0<=x_1<=A_1 that square is at least
(1-x_1)^2+beta^2*x_1^2, decreasing on this interval and minimized
at A_1. The displayed x_* attains it. Thus the TRUE closest radius is

    R_m^2=(1-A_1)^2+ell_2^2=(u_1^2+y_1^2)/M_1^2.               (4.4)

All original directed interpolation rows hold for this static function:
the1-smooth descent bound for V(z)=F(z)-<grad F(y),z>, minimized at y,
gives F(x)-F(y)-<grad F(y),x-y>>=||grad F(x)-grad F(y)||^2/2.
Both orientations, minimizers and terminal points are included. Actual
Euclidean Gram matrices are positive semidefinite by definition.

## 5. Uniform local estimates when beta=1/k

Let w_j=(y_j+beta)/(q_j+beta). At an uncapped point the circular normal
says u_j-beta=(p(q_j)-beta)w_j. A cross product in the backward step
gives

    w_(j-1)=w_j[1+
       (p(q_(j-1))*q_j-q_(j-1)*p(q_j))
                     /(beta*(p(q_(j-1))+q_(j-1)))].             (5.1)

When q_(j-1)=0 this identity follows from y_(j-1)=y_j; if both q are
zero it is equality. Its numerator is the integral from q_(j-1) to q_j
of p(q_(j-1))-q_(j-1)*p'(t). Dividing by p(q_(j-1))+q_(j-1)
bounds the integrand by max(1,-p'(t)). Since w_N=1, multiplication
and 1+z<=exp(z) give

    w_0<=exp(I_beta/beta),
    I_beta=integral_0^1 max(1,-p'(t))dt
             =sqrt(2)(1+beta)-beta<2.

The integral is evaluated by splitting where -p'(t)=1, namely
t=(1+beta)/sqrt(2)-beta. Therefore

    y_0<=2exp(2k)=:Y_k.                                         (5.2)

At any uncapped point u_j<=beta+2k(Y_k+beta). Uncapped backward
steps form a contiguous portion, whose total u increment is at most
2k(Y_k+beta); their p is at least beta. Capped steps have p=C_beta.
It follows from(3.4) that

    u_0=beta+C_beta*s/2-E_gap,
    0<=E_gap<=4k^2(Y_k+beta),
    |M-C_beta*s/2|<=20k^2 exp(2k)=:K_k.                         (5.3)

The last bound pays beta,y_0/beta,E_gap; the loose constant20 works
for k>=4. A useful exact lower bound comes from the concave function
p(q)+q/beta. It is at least the smaller endpoint value C_beta, since
beta+1/beta>=C_beta for beta<=1/4. Thus

    M>=beta+1/beta+C_beta*s/2>=C_beta*(1+s/2).                   (5.4)

The mass recurrence S_(j+1)=2S_j+b_j has the exact solution

    d_m=c-2, v_mass=a_word/d_m in(0,1),
    1+S_j=v_mass*c^j+(1-v_mass)*2^j,
    b_j=d_m*S_j+c-1-d_m*(1-v_mass)*2^j.                        (5.5)

The inequality v_mass<1 follows from c(c-2)>1>eta_word. We have
v_mass->1 and c->lambda. Uniformly for J<=j<=m-1, S_j/(v_mass*c^j)
has error O(c^(-J)+(2/c)^J) from1. Furthermore

    k*K_k/S_J->0,
    S_J/lambda^J->exp(1/4),
    S_(m-1)/lambda^(m-1)->exp(1/4).                             (5.6)

The numerator in the first limit has logarithm O(k+log k), while the
denominator has logarithm J log(lambda)+O(1), and k/m->0. The other
limits follow from 0<=x-log(1+x)<=x^2/2 for x>=0 and j/m->1.
These are symbolic comparisons; no recurrence or numeric evaluation
was executed.

## 6. Amplitudes, cap ratios, and completion of the scale screen

The first transfer is T_1/M_1; later transfers use the one-level-shifted
gap in(1.1). Equations(5.3)--(5.6) give

    D_(k+1)=[d_m/C_beta]*[c*d_m/C_beta]^(k-1)*(1+o(1)).         (6.1)

The errors are summable: each normalization relative error is
O(K_k/S_J) and each mass or subtraction-of1 error is
O(c^(-J)+(2/c)^J); k times their sum tends to zero. Taking logarithms
proves the product assertion. Since c*d_m=1+O(1/m), its(k-1)st power
tends to1. Also log(beta+sqrt(1+2beta))/beta->2, so C_beta^k->exp(2).
Consequently

    D_(k+1)->(lambda-2)exp(-2),
    R_m^2->1, sigma_1~2/(C_beta*S_J).                           (6.2)

The radius conclusion uses u_1~C_beta*S_J/2,y_1<=Y_k and
M_1~C_beta*S_J/2. The next ordinary block after selected checkpoint i
is exactly H_(J+i-1), so uniformly for i<k,

    sigma_(i+1)/sigma_i=T_i/M_(i+1)->lambda-2.                  (6.3)

Eventually all these ratios are between1/3 and1/2. Hence

    sum_i sigma_i^2<=2sigma_1^2,
    B_(n_m)*sigma_1^2/R_m^2->0,                                (6.4)

the second quantity being O(lambda^(-m+2k)). Combining(6.2), the suffix
mass limit, and(1.2) proves(1.4). At the comparison point in Section2,
x_1<=A_1 gives the exact edge expression
Phi_1=Hub_((1+beta^2)sigma_1)(beta*x_1-x_2)/(1+beta^2), derived
also in Section7. At(0,-1) this equals the value in Section2 for large
m, while later inputs lie below their thresholds with nonnegative
outputs. This proves(2.2).

For reference T_infty<1. The j=0,b=1 enclosure of accepted
fact04219554e41d9750 gives Z_1<5, since B_1=G(1,1)=4 and
(2+3/100)^2<5. As exp(17/4)>16 and 2lambda>4, T_infty<5/64.
Its deficit from1 must be supplied by retained values for a benchmark
witness from this policy. The scale screen does not supply them.

## 7. The entire actual retired system and its two constraint residuals

Index the final suffix by t=0,...,N, N=2^(m-1)-1, with steps h_t
equal to the ordered H_(m-1). Write x(t) for its first k coordinates.
The incoming vector x(0) is the ACTUAL vector obtained from every
earlier update(4.2). After subsequent checkpoints, retired coordinates
may have changed in both directions. We do NOT set all of them to
their individual post-checkpoint values A_i-beta D_(i+1) at the root.

Throughout the suffix Q_k=0, and the fixed retired function is

    G_ret(x)=1/2 sum_(i<k) E_(sigma_i K)
                       (x_i-A_i,x_(i+1)-ell_(i+1))
                         +1/2 Hub_(C_beta*sigma_k)(x_k-A_k).    (7.1)

The last circular component is horizontal because its output is above
ell_(k+1). Its value is retained exactly, not dropped. The full retired
update is

    x_i(t+1)=x_i(t)-(h_t/2)[P_i(t)-Q_(i-1)(t)], 1<=i<=k,        (7.2)

where P_k is the derivative of the Huber in(7.1), and V_m=2G_ret(x(N)).
For i<k define

    r_i=x_i-A_i, z_i=beta(x_i-ell_i)-x_(i+1),
    U_i=P_i-beta Q_i>=0.

Also r_k=x_k-A_k,U_k=P_k,Q_0=Q_k=0. U_i is a horizontal cone
coefficient, distinct from the original benchmark U_n^ZJ. Since
beta A_i=beta ell_i+ell_(i+1), the local envelope is exactly

    Phi_i=max {U*r_i+Q*z_i-[(U+beta Q)^2+Q^2]/2:
            0<=Q<=sigma_i,
            0<=U<=sigma_i*p(Q/sigma_i)-beta Q}.                (7.3)

Indeed, the horizontal sections of K run from p=beta q to p=p(q).
The lower segment joins0 to(beta,-1), the upper boundary is concave,
and their convex hull contains every intervening point. Concavity also
bounds the convex hull above. Thus(7.3) is an identity.

When r_i<=0, maximizing first over U gives U_i=0, followed by

    Q_i=clip(z_i/(1+beta^2),0,sigma_i),
    Phi_i=Hub_((1+beta^2)sigma_i)(z_i)/(1+beta^2).               (7.4)

For general r_i, U_i can be positive. The exact residual updates are

    r_i^+=r_i-(h/2)(U_i+beta Q_i-Q_(i-1)),
    z_i^+=z_i-(h/2)[(1+beta^2)Q_i-beta Q_(i-1)-beta Q_(i+1)
                                               +beta U_i-U_(i+1)]. (7.5)

These include both neighboring contributions. Summing independent
two-Huber tail laws would delete genuine terms of(7.5).

## 8. Whole-hierarchy comparison with all input excursions charged

Put kappa=1+beta^2,theta=(1-beta)/(1+beta), and for i<k define

    qhat_i=clip(z_i/kappa,0,sigma_i), qhat_0=qhat_k=0,
    barsigma_i=sigma_i for 1<=i<k, barsigma_0=barsigma_k=0,
    e_i=beta*(barsigma_(i-1)+barsigma_(i+1)),
    gamma_i=kappa*sigma_i-e_i.

Here e_i,gamma_i are fixed cap combinations. From(6.3) and beta->0,
eventually gamma_i>0 and, uniformly for i<k,

    d_m*e_i<=gamma_i.                                         (8.1)

Every exact maximizer in(7.3) satisfies

    Q_i<=qhat_i,
    kappa(qhat_i-Q_i)-beta U_i<=theta U_i.                     (8.2)

For the complete curved-regime check, the function
A(q)=sigma_i*p(q/sigma_i)-beta q decreases continuously from
C_beta*sigma_i to0. At fixed U its feasible Q interval is[0,qmax(U)],
where A(qmax(U))=U and qmax(0)=sigma_i. The maximizer therefore has
Q=clip((z-beta U)/kappa,0,qmax(U)), proving Q<=qhat. If the upper
cap is inactive, kappa(qhat-Q)<=beta U. If active, Q=qmax(U).
Concavity and endpoint values imply p(q)+q>=1+beta, hence
U=A(Q)>=(1+beta)(sigma_i-Q). It follows that
kappa(qhat-Q)-beta U<=kappa(sigma_i-Q)-beta U<=theta U.
Lower and upper cap boundaries are included.

Insert(8.2) into(7.5). All neighbor differences that are dropped are
NONPOSITIVE, since Q_j<=qhat_j. The resulting unconditional step bound is

    z_i^+<=z_i-(h/2)[kappa qhat_i-beta qhat_(i-1)-beta qhat_(i+1)]
                                  +(h/2)[theta U_i+U_(i+1)].  (8.3)

Define the nonnegative charge from the ACTUAL suffix trajectory

    L_i=(1/2)sum_(t=0)^(N-1) h_t[theta U_i(t)+U_(i+1)(t)].     (8.4)

For any starting vector, the final residual obeys

    z_i(N)<=max{kappa*sigma_i+(c-1)e_i/2,
                              z_i(0)-gamma_i*S_(m-1)/2}+L_i.  (8.5)

If z_i(N)<=kappa*sigma_i this is immediate. Otherwise take its LAST
entry into z_i>kappa*sigma_i. If it was above the threshold at all
samples, qhat_i=sigma_i throughout, and summing(8.3) with the neighbor
caps gives the second term plus L_i. Otherwise the last entry uses an
actual step b_j,0<=j<m-1, whose preceding z_i is at most kappa*sigma_i.
Dropping only its nonpositive own term bounds the new residual by
kappa*sigma_i+b_j e_i/2 plus that step's charge. Immediately after
this occurrence is a complete H_j within the remaining suffix: this
right-subtree property follows inductively from the word definition.
At all samples after the last entry, qhat_i=sigma_i. The following
H_j therefore loses at least gamma_i*S_j/2 apart from its charges;
all further steps lose a nonnegative amount apart from their charges.
The uncharged bound is kappa*sigma_i+(b_j e_i-gamma_i S_j)/2.
Using(5.5), b_j<=d_m S_j+c-1, and(8.1) proves the first term.
Adding at most L_i pays EVERY source, including the crossing and all
large steps. This is an argument on the ordered hierarchy, not a
mass-only replacement of its nonlinear dynamics.

For this policy's ACTUAL incoming vector the second term in(8.5) is
nonpositive for all sufficiently large m. Let S_pre=S_(m-1)+b_(m-1),
the complete mass before the suffix. Global updates(4.2) and caps give

    delta_(i1)-C_beta*sigma_i*S_pre/2<=x_i(0)
                    <=delta_(i1)+sigma_(i-1)*S_pre/2,

where sigma_0=0 and delta_(i1) is1 for i=1 and0 otherwise. Therefore

    z_i(0)<=beta*delta_(i1)
                   +(beta*sigma_(i-1)+C_beta*sigma_(i+1))*S_pre/2. (8.6)

Only the nonpositive -beta ell_i was dropped. Uniformly in i, the
coefficient divided by sigma_i tends to lambda-2,
S_pre/S_(m-1)->lambda-1, and gamma_i/sigma_i->1. Their product
tends to (lambda-2)(lambda-1)=2-sqrt(2)<1. For i=1 the extra ratio
beta/(sigma_1*S_(m-1)) tends to0 by(6.2) and J=m-k. This proves
the incoming budget. As kappa+(c-1)e_i/(2sigma_i)->1 uniformly,
(8.5) yields eventually

    (z_i(N))_+<=2sigma_i+L_i.                                 (8.7)

There is no assumption that earlier components were zero at the root.
The exact maximization formula also gives

    Phi_i<=C_beta*sigma_i*(r_i)_++sigma_i*(z_i)_+, i<k,
    Phi_k<=C_beta*sigma_k*(r_k)_+.

Define one remaining nonnegative quantity on the ACTUAL trajectory:

    X_m=C_beta*sum_(i=1)^k sigma_i*(r_i(N))_+
           +(1/2)sum_(t=0)^(N-1) h_t
                         sum_(i=1)^(k-1) sigma_i
                                      [theta U_i(t)+U_(i+1)(t)]. (8.8)

Summing(8.7) and using(6.4) proves the unconditional comparison

    0<=V_m<=4sigma_1^2+X_m,
    B_(n_m)E_m<=T_m+o(1)+B_(n_m)X_m/R_m^2.                    (8.9)

Every old value is included. X_m has NOT been proved small. It is a
sufficient interface, not an equivalent formula for V_m: accumulated
nonnegative charges can overcount activity dissipated later. Proving
B_(n_m)X_m/R_m^2->0 would imply the complete scoped obstruction
B_(n_m)E_m->T_infty<1. No such estimate is asserted here.

## 9. The conditional slab result and a counterexample to blanket invariance

If every suffix sample satisfied x_i(t)<=A_i for all i<=k, then
U_i(t)=0 by(7.3)--(7.4), and every terminal(r_i)_+=0. Thus X_m=0
and(8.9) proves a full asymptotic obstruction. This is CONDITIONAL,
not a property proved for the actual trajectory. It already concerns
the coupled edge system, with no independence assumption.

The entire slab {x_i<=A_i for 1<=i<=k} is NOT invariant for this
same static function and the available large steps. For all large m,
consider this suffix state in the original R^(k+1):

    x_1=A_1, x_2=ell_2-kappa*sigma_1, x_3=ell_3,
    x_4=...=x_k=0, x_(k+1)=ell_(k+1)+D_(k+1).

All retired inputs are at most their A_i. The first component has
z_1=kappa*sigma_1 and(P_1,Q_1)=(beta*sigma_1,sigma_1).
The second has input<A_2 and output ell_3, so P_2=Q_2=0. Later
circular components vanish: the third input is its threshold ell_3
and its output0; subsequent inputs are0 with nonnegative outputs.
The terminal scalar does not affect coordinate2. Therefore

    x_2^+=ell_2-kappa*sigma_1+h*sigma_1/2>A_2
       whenever h>2kappa+2ell_3/(beta*sigma_1).                 (9.1)

The unchanged suffix contains h=b_(m-2). Meanwhile
ell_3/(beta*sigma_1)=T_1*y_2/(beta*M_2)=O(k exp(2k)) by
(5.2),(5.4),(5.5), whereas b_(m-2) grows like lambda^(m-2).
Thus(9.1) holds eventually. This uses the SAME function and a physical
step in the suffix; it is not a construction repair.

The falsifying state is NOT asserted to be the actual state at that
step. It refutes blanket slab invariance only. A trajectory-specific
barrier or a bound on the excursion flux could still hold. The example
identifies why a two-checkpoint absorption argument cannot be applied
independently to every pair.

## 10. Exact energy accounting and the remaining interfaces

An exact identity also keeps the signed effects that the sufficient
positive-source bound(8.8) may overcount. Let z_*=(A_1,ell_2,0,...,0)
in R^k, which minimizes G_ret, and define at each suffix sample

    d_i=P_i-Q_(i-1),
    N_grad=sum_i P_i^2+sum_(i<k)Q_i^2,
    C_cross=sum_(i=2)^k P_i Q_(i-1),
    slack=sum_(i<k)[-<(P_i,-Q_i),
                        (z_*(i)-A_i,z_*(i+1)-ell_(i+1))>]
                                     -P_k*(z_*(k)-A_k)>=0.

The slack sign follows from the polar-cone condition at a minimizer;
it is not an assumed sign for individual coordinates. The maximization
identity gives <grad G_ret(x),x-z_*>=G_ret(x)+N_grad/4+slack/2.
Since sum_i d_i^2=N_grad-2C_cross, expansion of(7.2) gives for EVERY
physical step, with no restriction on its size,

    ||x-z_*||^2-||x^+-z_*||^2
       =2h G_ret(x)+h(2-h)N_grad/4
                         +h^2 C_cross/2+h*slack.              (10.1)

C_cross and slack are nonnegative. The N_grad coefficient is negative
when h>2 and MUST be paid. Dropping it would not be a descent proof.

For the exact terminal value, write
D_E(v,u)=E(v)-E(u)-<grad E(u),v-u>>=0 for the Bregman remainder of
a differentiable convex E. For i<k take the fixed envelope E_(sigma_i K)
at its two centered coordinates, and for i=k take Hub_(C_beta*sigma_k)
at r_k. Let B_i(t) denote the remainder from sample t to t+1. Direct
first-order expansion of(7.1) gives the equality

    V_m=2G_ret(x(0))
              -(1/2)sum_t h_t sum_i d_i(t)^2
                                      +sum_t sum_i B_i(t).    (10.2)

All overlaps enter through d_i, and all overshoots remain in the actual
Bregman terms. Neither sum has been compared sharply enough here.

A sufficient retained contribution for a diagnostic lower witness at
the original benchmark is, for example,

    liminf_m B_(n_m)*V_m/R_m^2>=1-T_infty.                     (10.3)

Its exact V_m is specified by the actual static trajectory or by(10.2).
A strict version would give a strict margin for THIS diagnostic family.
A strict upper bound for the same term below1-T_infty would instead
obstruct this policy. The explicit sufficient unpaid obstruction
inequality supplied by the new comparison is

    B_(n_m)*X_m/R_m^2 ->0.                                   (10.4)

Attempt one: obtain(10.4) from slab invariance. Section9 refutes general
invariance; actual-trajectory invariance remains unproved. Attempt two:
use distance descent. Equation(10.1) exposes the unpaid h(2-h) term.
Its favorable coupled cross term is exact, but no comparison paying
all large-step negative terms from it has been established.

No snapshot, independent pair sum, or terminal-only product is offered
as the result. Equations(1.3),(8.8)--(8.9),(10.2)--(10.4) state exactly
what is paid and what is not. This round does NOT complete a retained
terminal bound for the chosen policy.

Even a successful(10.3) only treats H_(m,1/4) at horizons2^m-1.
The original all-word/all-horizon minimax question would still require
a lower bound for W_n(h) uniform over EVERY nonnegative word h of
length n, at the original B_n scale, or another valid route to the
original target. No such extension follows from this one policy.

## 11. Retrieval, resources, and handoff

Query-memory recalled the shifted-circle construction, complete
two-checkpoint draft, scoped scalar/tree failures, and current guidance.
Fact searches found no accepted growing circular-prefix result.
Fact5fd396eba75651e7 concerns different spiral families and is not a
premise. Only the original-ray conclusion of04219554e41d9750 is used;
its statement and relevant proof were read.

Matlas retrieval for overlapping projection envelopes and long-step
retained Huber transport returned three non-applicable results:
2011.14269 Lemma3.6 is a continuous-flow perturbation bound;
2212.04459 Lemma3 is a proximal descent inequality;2012.15458 Lemma2.1
concerns a different Moreau composition. None was imported.

The exact primary source was downloaded and extracted:
Kaizhao Liu and Yuhan Ye, Silver Rate Is (Almost) Optimal for Gradient

# R8 matched-child / auxiliary-mixture handoff

**UNVERIFIED. Original target OPEN. No full-class certificate or formal-review candidate.**

Allocation `e72c37e67efa7a9a` used one native analytical invocation and two substantive proof lines. Mathematical programs, arrays, solvers, formal submissions, new agents/models and consults were zero. Parent owns ledger closeout. No automatic renewal.

The complete derivations and source audit are in `PROOF_ATTEMPT.md`. The same enclosing m>=1536 is used in every child of H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j), with epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon), a=beta/c. All physical and auxiliary projections use one compact convex body K containing zero.

Accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` supplies full-class finite-word projection-simplex universality and the actual H_3 U law. It does not supply higher primitive signs. Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50, D_m=1+2S_m. A later proven full W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) for all sufficiently large m would still close the negative route via the strict factor799/800. This invocation does not supply that premise.

## Line one: matched original normal rows

For the actual left/right copies write x_i,y_i and g_i=P(x_i), f_i=P(y_i), with y_0=x_N-b g_N. Let delta_i=x_i-y_i and e_i=g_i-f_i. The original matched normal sum is

    mu_i=<delta_i,e_i>-||e_i||^2>=0,
    ||delta_0||^2-||delta_N||^2
      =sum h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2+2sum h_i mu_i.

The original bridge boundaries are delta_0=sum h_i g_i+b g_N and delta_N=sum h_i f_i+b g_N. Combining the exact telescope with the accepted actual-root U identity leaves the fully specified unpaid term (3.6): a boundary bilinear expression, the internal long-step squares with negative coefficients, and minus the actual right radial and maximization remainders. No quantitative payment by the retained root, matched rows, and actual right U gap was obtained.

The tempting difference-space child argument is invalid without an additional proof. Its putative projection row equals

    S_ij=Q_ij^(L,L)+Q_ij^(R,R)-Q_ij^(R,L)-Q_ij^(L,R)-mu_j.

Every original Q is nonpositive, but the negative cross coefficients prevent a sign for S_ij. Thus the differences are not assumed to form a gradient history of another convex function. Every original off-diagonal cross row remains available; no impossibility result for all their multipliers is claimed.

## Line two: an actually feasible parent auxiliary mixture

For child mass s, C=1+s, b=1+k, set A=1+2s, D=d+2s, h=C+k, M=D+2h. Keep p=P(v), w=v-bp, r=P(w/D), l=P(x/A), z_L=[x-v+C p]/A, u=l-z_L. Let z_P be the accepted actual prefix mixture. The proposed point is

    z_theta=z_P+(theta A/M)u,
    M z_theta=(1-theta)sum h_i g_i+(b-theta C)p
                 +(D+h)r+theta A l.

The displayed convex mixture is feasible for 0<=theta<=b/C<1 at the relevant levels. The exact parent gap is equation (4.5), retaining the actual child U gaps, positive root reserve, and nonnegative parent maximization remainder. The child-gap signs are accepted at j=3 and would require the proposed induction at higher j; they are not higher-depth premises. Its remaining signed term is

    T_theta=-(1-theta)^2 A^2||u||^2
              -2(1-theta)A<x-A l,u>
              +2theta A<(s-k)p-(D+h)r,u>.

The first normal is actual and nonnegative, but has a negative coefficient here. Setting theta=1 would cancel the earlier left-maximization debt, yet would give the bridge gradient the negative mixture weight b-C=k-s. No same-body membership proof for that extrapolated point was obtained. Completing squares only against the root ||p-r||^2 leaves an uncontrolled r component, since

    (s-k)p-(D+h)r=(s-k)(p-r)-(D+1+2k)r.

The actual child gaps and remainders cannot be assigned independent values. This is a failed quantitative payment, not a U counterexample or a proof that coupled cross rows cannot work.

## Recovery and stopping boundary

The root coefficient Theta is positive at every relevant higher level: the report proves k/C<=c-2<1/2 and Theta>3C(C+k)/46 for j>=2. That observation is not a closure. Any continuation of these exact constructions would have to estimate the complete unpaid expression with the actual same-body cross rows; merely postulating its sign would restate the missing argument. No new finite-horizon toy result, scalar q_j profile, fixed-source fan, or unsupported higher primitive positivity should be counted as progress from this file.

All required public sources were read, including the complete accepted Part B/C and both warned-against historical strengthenings. No private worker memory or raw mathematical arrays were accessed. No service, worker, truth store, publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown, or Goal action occurred. Stop here for parent full-read and allocation closeout.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000683 -->
# R8: actual matched-child rows and an auxiliary-mixture attempt

**UNVERIFIED. No all-depth upper bound or original-target resolution was obtained.**

Allocation `e72c37e67efa7a9a`: one native analytical invocation, two substantive proof lines, mathematical programs/arrays/solvers/formal submissions zero. The parent owns the allocation ledger. This report records exact manual identities and the unpaid terms in the two attempted certificates. It does not assert a counterexample to the U inequality or to the proposed final objective bound.

## 1. Target, fixed hierarchy, and accepted premises

For a predetermined nonnegative word h of length n, the original quantity is

    W_n(h) = sup 2(f(x_n)-f(x_star))/(L ||x_0-x_star||^2),
    x_(i+1) = x_i - (h_i/L) grad f(x_i).

The supremum includes every finite Euclidean dimension, every convex differentiable L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every selected minimizer, and every different start. Write W_n^* = inf_(h>=0) W_n(h), and U_n^ZJ = 1/B_n for the exact original all-integer-split dominant benchmark. The normalization is W = 2R. The original question is whether W_n^*/U_n^ZJ tends to one.

Fix an enclosing integer m >= 1536, and throughout all children use

    lambda = 1+sqrt(2), epsilon = 1/(4m), beta = 1-epsilon,
    c = lambda(1+epsilon), a = beta/c,
    H_0 = (), H_(j+1) = (H_j, 1+a c^j, H_j),
    S_j = sum H_j, C_j = 1+S_j, D_m = 1+2S_m.

Each H_j is the original strictly positive word of length 2^j-1. No child resets epsilon or m. Accepted fact `931b9653ce0f7201` gives B_(2^m-1)/D_m < 47/50. Consequently a full-class bound W_(2^m-1)(H_m) <= 17/(16D_m) for every sufficiently large m would give a strict asymptotic separation, since (17/16)(47/50) = 799/800 < 1. That upper bound is not proved here.

The accepted facts used from `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` are exactly these:

1. For each fixed finite word, the full original supremum equals the supremum over origin-vertex projection-simplex envelopes. This retains every original selected minimizer, or equivalently the closest polar distance for positive scores. It is equality of suprema, not an exact uniformly conditioned lift of each original run.
2. For every nonempty compact convex set K containing zero, every start x, and every d >= 1, the actual same-m H_3 satisfies U_d(T_H3 x) <= U_(d+2S_3)(x), with all projections on one K.
3. Complete Part B provides the actual-child identities reproduced below. Neither positivity of the primitive energy for H_j with j >= 3 nor an all-depth U law is an accepted premise.

Here and below K denotes the projection body; D is a scalar trailing parameter. Put

    P(z) = P_K(z),
    F(z) = max_(g in K) (<z,g>-||g||^2/2),
    U_t(z) = max_(g in K) (2<z,g>-t||g||^2),  t>0.

The actual gradient of F is P. Its full minimum set is the polar cone

    K^polar = {z : <z,g> <= 0 for all g in K}.

For any selected x_star in this cone, U_t(x) <= ||x-x_star||^2/t. Thus a proved all-body U law at d=1 would transfer to the full W supremum using the accepted finite-word universality. None of the manipulations below changes the physical initial radius, terminal, dimension quantifier, or word.

## 2. Common actual parent data and the retained root identity

Consider the actual parent H_(j+1), with child mass s = S_j, capacity C = 1+s, bridge b = 1+k, and k = a c^j. Use N = 2^j-1, and write both complete child histories as

    x_0 = x, x_(i+1) = x_i - h_i g_i, g_i = P(x_i), x_N = v, p = g_N,
    y_0 = w = v-bp,
    y_(i+1) = y_i - h_i f_i, f_i = P(y_i), y_N = y, q = f_N.

The same h_i is used at matched indices in the two actual copies. The notation f_i in this section is a gradient vector, not a function value. The two starts are linked by the displayed physical bridge. They are never varied independently.

For a trailing d >= 1 define

    D = d+2s, h = s+b = C+k, M = D+2h,
    r = P(w/D), q_d = P(y/d), p_0 = P(x/M),
    z_R = [w-y+(d+s)q_d]/D,
    z_P = [x-w+(D+h)r]/M.

In particular r is simultaneously the prefix's terminal auxiliary projection and the right child's initial auxiliary projection. It is one actual point, not two independently selected optimizers.

For any t>0, z in K, and l=P(u/t), define

    Rem(u,t,z) = U_t(u) - [2<u,z>-t||z||^2]
               = t||l-z||^2 + 2<u-tl,l-z> >= 0.

The sign follows from the normal inequality <u-tl,z-l> <= 0. If z is a convex mixture of sampled gradients, the second term is a nonnegative combination of their original same-K normal rows. Put RP = Rem(x,M,z_P), RR = Rem(w,D,z_R), and

    V_L = ||x||^2 - ||v+s p||^2,
    V_R = ||w||^2 - ||y+s q||^2,
    LR = 2s<y,q-q_d> + s^2(||q||^2-||q_d||^2) >= 0.

The last sign follows by comparing the optimizers defining U_1(y) and U_d(y). It does not involve a new physical step. Also define

    Np = <v-p,p-r> >= 0,
    Nr = <w-D r,r-p> >= 0,
    Theta = h[CD-k(C+D+k)]/(D+k),
    J = Theta||p-r||^2 + 2h(1+C/(D+k))Np + 2hC/(D+k)Nr.

The accepted Part B identities, with these actual data, give

    E_R := U_D(w)-U_d(y) = (V_R+LR)/D + RR,               (2.1)
    Psi := U_M(x)-U_d(y) = V_L/M + J/M + RP + E_R.       (2.2)

Neither equality assumes V_L >= 0 or V_R >= 0.

The scalar root coefficient itself remains positive at every level relevant to these attempts. Indeed the exact mass recurrence has the solution

    C_j = v_0 c^j + (1-v_0)2^j,
    v_0 = a/(c-2) = beta/[c(c-2)],  0<v_0<1.

This follows by substitution into C_(j+1)=2C_j+a c^j and C_0=1. Since c(c-2)>lambda(lambda-2)=1>beta, the displayed range for v_0 holds. Hence k/C <= c-2. To bound c, sqrt(2)<10/7 gives

    c < (17/7)(6145/6144) < 5/2;

the final comparison is 208930 < 215040 after multiplication by positive denominators. Thus k<C/2. For j>=2, C>5 and D>=2C-1>9C/5. As in the accepted root calculation,

    Theta/h = C-k-2Ck/(D+k) > C/2-10C/23 = 3C/46,
    Theta > 3C(C+k)/46 > 0.                            (2.3)

Also a>1/3, because beta>9/10 and c<5/2 give a>9/25>1/3; this verifies C_2=4+2a+beta>5. The positive root reserve is therefore available. The unresolved matter is payment of V_L, not the sign of Theta.

## 3. Proof line one: original matched rows, with the actual boundary retained

Set

    delta_i = x_i-y_i, e_i = g_i-f_i, 0<=i<=N.

The exact update is delta_(i+1)=delta_i-h_i e_i. The two original matched normal reserves are

    A_i = <x_i-g_i,g_i-f_i> >= 0,
    B_i = <y_i-f_i,f_i-g_i> >= 0,
    mu_i = A_i+B_i = <delta_i,e_i>-||e_i||^2 >= 0.

Expanding the squared update and summing every physical index gives

    ||delta_0||^2-||delta_N||^2
      = sum_i h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2 + 2 sum_i h_i mu_i. (3.1)

The boundary is the actual one:

    delta_0 = sum_i h_i g_i + b p,
    delta_N = sum_i h_i f_i + b p.                    (3.2)

Equations (3.1)-(3.2) retain all matched rows and both complete copies. In particular they do not prove arbitrary two-start nonexpansivity. Already the physical level-two bridge has h_i=1+beta c>2, since beta c=lambda(1-epsilon^2)>1. Thus H_j for j>=3 contains strictly negative coefficients h_i(2-h_i) in (3.1).

For clarity about the boundary terms, put e_N=p-q and define

    Bdy = 2<w,delta_0>
          -2<y+s q,delta_N+s e_N>
          -2s<delta_N,e_N>-s^2||e_N||^2.             (3.3)

Direct expansion of the definitions of V_L and V_R gives

    V_L = V_R + ||delta_0||^2-||delta_N||^2 + Bdy.   (3.4)

This expansion uses x=w+delta_0 and v+s p=y+s q+delta_N+s e_N; hence no endpoint has been substituted. Combining (2.1)-(2.2) with (3.1)-(3.4) yields the exact attempted coupled payment

    M Psi = (M+D)E_R + J + M RP + 2 sum_i h_i mu_i
            + Bdy + sum_i h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2
            - LR - D RR.                           (3.5)

If an induction supplied the right-child U law, E_R would be nonnegative. At j=3 that sign is actually supplied by the accepted base. The remaining actual unpaid expression in this construction is

    T_match = Bdy + sum_i h_i(2-h_i)||e_i||^2
              - LR - D RR.                         (3.6)

No estimate proving that J+M RP+2 sum h_i mu_i+(M+D)E_R pays the negative part of (3.6) was obtained. In particular, replacing LR and RR by independent favorable values would reverse their role in (3.5). They are the actual right-child remainders with a negative sign in this attempted transfer. Keeping their correlation is necessary.

### Why a difference-space child certificate is not a payment

All original off-diagonal cross rows remain available. To state their exact orientation, for a in {L,R} set z_i^L=x_i, z_i^R=y_i, g_i^L=g_i, g_i^R=f_i and

    Q_(i,j)^(a,b) = <z_j^b-g_j^b, g_i^a-g_j^b> <= 0.

The formal projection row one might try to assign to the differences is

    S_ij = <delta_j-e_j, e_i-e_j>.

Expansion in the original samples gives exactly

    S_ij = Q_ij^(L,L) + Q_ij^(R,R)
           - Q_ij^(R,L) - Q_ij^(L,R)
           + Q_jj^(R,L) + Q_jj^(L,R)
         = Q_ij^(L,L) + Q_ij^(R,R)
           - Q_ij^(R,L) - Q_ij^(L,R) - mu_j.        (3.7)

Although every Q on the right is nonpositive, the two negative coefficients prevent a conclusion S_ij<=0. Thus the paired update and cocoercivity do not establish that (delta_i,e_i) is a gradient history of one smooth convex function or a projection history of one body. The complete child certificate cannot be applied in that fictitious space. Equation (3.7) explicitly records the extra cross rows that such a step would have to pay; they were not silently discarded.

The sign difficulties in (3.5)-(3.7) are a failure of this proposed proof. They are not an admissible function counterexample to U, and they do not rule out a different nonnegative combination of the full original cross rows.

## 4. Proof line two: mix the actual left auxiliary optimizer into the parent

The second attempt replaces the unpaid V_L by the actual left U gap minus its maximization remainder, and tries to pay that remainder through a feasible parent comparison point. It uses the original same-body optimizers rather than an independently reset child maximizer.

Put

    A = 1+2s,
    l = P(x/A), z_L = [x-v+C p]/A,
    u = l-z_L, n_L = x-A l,
    RL = Rem(x,A,z_L) = A||u||^2+2<n_L,u> >= 0,
    E_L = U_A(x)-U_1(v).

Both z_L and l belong to the same K. Since x-A z_L=v-Cp, one has

    RL = 2<v-Cp,u>-A||u||^2,
    V_L = A(E_L-RL).                               (4.1)

The second equality is the exact child maximization identity at trailing one. If the left child U induction were valid, E_L would be nonnegative; at j=3 this is the accepted base. No sign for V_L is inferred.

For a parameter theta choose the candidate feasible parent point

    z_theta = z_P + (theta A/M)u.

Writing G=x-v=sum_i h_i g_i gives its complete gradient mixture:

    M z_theta = (1-theta)G + (b-theta C)p
                +(D+h)r + theta A l.               (4.2)

The coefficients sum to M. Therefore the following explicit range certifies membership in K using only convexity and the original samples:

    0 <= theta <= min(1,b/C).                        (4.3)

For j>=2, k<C/2 and C>5 imply b<C, so this range has theta<=b/C<1. Equation (4.3) is a sufficient range for this convex-mixture construction; it is not asserted to characterize membership for every particular body. Extending it would require an actual membership proof using the coupled history.

Let R_theta=Rem(x,M,z_theta)>=0 in the range (4.3). The difference of the two feasible variational values is obtained by one square expansion:

    M { [2<x,z_theta>-M||z_theta||^2]
        -[2<x,z_P>-M||z_P||^2] }
      = 2theta A<w-(D+h)r,u> - theta^2 A^2||u||^2.  (4.4)

Use

    w-(D+h)r = v-Cp + (s-k)p-(D+h)r

and (4.1). Combining the result with (2.2) gives the exact same-body identity

    M Psi = A E_L + M E_R + J + M R_theta + T_theta, (4.5)

where the unpaid term is

    T_theta = -(1-theta)A RL
              +theta(1-theta)A^2||u||^2
              +2theta A<(s-k)p-(D+h)r,u>.           (4.6)

Equivalently, using the actual normal n_L,

    T_theta = -(1-theta)^2 A^2||u||^2
              -2(1-theta)A<n_L,u>
              +2theta A<(s-k)p-(D+h)r,u>.           (4.7)

Here <n_L,u> >= 0 is itself the actual maximization normal reserve. Thus it occurs with a negative coefficient in (4.7). The mixed term has no sign from the rows already used. None of E_L, E_R, R_theta, p, r or u may be chosen independently to make it favorable.

The value theta=1 would remove the explicit left-remainder coefficient in (4.6), but (4.2) would give the bridge gradient weight b-C=k-s<0. Convexity of K then does not certify membership of that proposed point. This is a concrete feasibility limitation of this auxiliary mixture, not a claim that z_1 is outside every particular K. At the largest guaranteed feasible theta=b/C, the negative squared coefficient in (4.7) is still strictly present.

One can also write the mixed vector as

    (s-k)p-(D+h)r = (s-k)(p-r) -(D+1+2k)r.         (4.8)

The root square in J controls p-r. Completing a square against it alone leaves the r component in (4.8), along with the signed left normal in (4.7). I did not obtain an original-cross-row inequality controlling that component jointly with E_L, E_R and R_theta. In particular, a completion that treated u and r as free could produce a negative formal direction, but such a direction would not be a realizable counterexample: the actual projections and bridge impose additional constraints. No such formal direction is promoted to a statement about the full U law here.

This second attempt supplies an admissible family of parent comparison points and its exact unpaid term. It supplies neither a nonnegative certificate nor a controlled final loss. An assumption that the remainder in (4.5) is nonnegative would be the missing all-depth argument, not a completed recursion.

## 5. Consequence, audit, and bounded stopping record

The accepted H_3 U law survives both analyses. Its scalar root reserve remains positive at higher levels by (2.3), but that fact alone does not give a sign for either complete unpaid expression (3.6) or (4.6). The direct full-class target W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) therefore remains unproved. No competing full-class lower bound was derived. The original asymptotic minimax question remains OPEN.

The following scope checks were performed manually:

- Every index i in both children is retained, including the actual endpoints; the physical word length is never shortened.
- Both physical paths and every auxiliary optimizer use one fixed K. In particular the right initial r is exactly P(w/D).
- The bridge relation appears in both (3.2) and (4.2)-(4.4). Arbitrary two-start nonexpansivity is not an assumption.
- Difference vectors were not declared to be gradients of a new function. Their exact original-row expansion (3.7) shows the unproved extra sign.
- V_L and V_R are not assumed nonnegative. H_3 primitive positivity and the R7 all-level terminal potential are not premises.
- No old fixed-source fan, Green parametrization, raw array, solver result, or finite table is used.
- Neither a signed quadratic remainder nor an unproved coefficient sign is a function counterexample.
- No approximation, limit interchange, or normalization change is used to infer a full-class bound.

Read completely: the allocation TASK (SHA-256 `4aac7aea7322362f0ce321119a822313a6e842b43c287edfd769e774a2c4082c`), current OPERATOR, original PROBLEM, R7_DIRECT_HANDOFF, R7 relaxed-terminal PROOF_ATTEMPT, the statement and complete Parts B/C of accepted `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`, and the historical PUBLIC `R8_PRIOR_U_RECORD_fd4d21078037120b.md`. The latter's old H_2 gap is superseded by the accepted proof and was not reopened. Its shifted-terminal-norm counterexample was not re-proved. The statement of accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` was read for the precise conditional asymptotic implication. Initial combined read output was truncated; the relevant source portions were re-read in bounded calls before use. The memory registry was consulted only for target/evidence-scope consistency; current source files control every premise.

The mathematical-academic-writing skill and its mathematical-integrity/source-audit references were used to keep proof claims, exact identities, and unsupported signs distinct. No external mathematical theorem is imported into the new derivations, so no literature claim needs a new source.

The two allocated proof lines are exhausted. No mathematical programs, numerical arrays, solvers, formal submissions, further agents/models, paid consults, private worker memory, service/worker actions, truth-store edits, publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown, or Goal mutations occurred. File operations only read or wrote text and computed hashes. This invocation stops for the parent's complete read and closeout, with no automatic renewal.

# R8: full-class closure from actual coupled child projection remainders

Fresh ONE gpt-6-astra/ultra analytical invocation, <=TWO substantive proof lines, mathematical programs/arrays/solvers/formal submissions ZERO. Consult OFF, parent owns budget. Read OPERATOR.md, COMPLETE PROBLEM.md, research/R7_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md and the complete relevant accepted proof identified below. Human priority is direct original-target closure inspired by Zhang--Jiang, not equivalent models or fixed-horizon toy examples. All old budgets closed.

## Concrete full-class negative route

Original target is lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, W=2R, exact original all-integer-split B_n; both possible answers remain permitted. For enclosing m>=1536, set eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=(1+sqrt2)(1+eps), a=beta/c, H0 empty, H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), SAME parameters in every child. Let S_j=sum H_j, D_m=1+2S_m, n_m=2^m-1. Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 proves B_n/D_m<47/50. A full-class W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) for every sufficiently large m gives a strict asymptotic separation; its proof is currently missing.

Accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4 has two genuinely usable statements. Read fact_graph/facts/1e0bf3f41457d8e4.md statement and COMPLETE Proof Part B (and Part C), not just this summary:

1. For each fixed physical word, the full W supremum equals the restricted supremum over projection envelopes F_D(x)=max_(g in D)(<x,g>-||g||^2/2) for origin-vertex simplices, retaining original selected minima/closest polar distance. This is an equality of suprema, not a uniformly conditioned exact finite lift of every run.
2. For the actual seven-step H3 with these inherited parameters, ALL compact convex D containing0, ALL starts and ALL trailing d>=1 obey

    U_d(T_H3 x)<=U_(d+2S3)(x),
    U_d(x)=max_(g in D)(2<x,g>-d||g||^2).

All physical and auxiliary projections use ONE fixed D. This yields exact full W7=1/(1+2S3), but no all-depth law is accepted.

The direct target is to propagate a genuine U inequality or a controlled loss whose FINAL consequence is the full W<=17/(16D_m). Do not merely produce an abstract recursion requiring the same missing inequality. Original physical length2^m-1, actual terminal, all starts/bodies, and full polar minimum cone are mandatory.

## Exact retained structure and the specific proposed new ingredient

Part B of the accepted proof gives an exact actual-left/bridge identity with V_left of unknown sign, a positive root auxiliary square, physical/auxiliary normal reserves, and maximization remainders. Its H3 proof uses complete H2 child energies and explicitly avoids assuming that H3 itself is primitive. At higher levels, it is invalid simply to assume V_left>=0. The R7 all-level terminal potential with q_j=(1+eps)^(-j) has a REAL asymmetric-Huber counterexample at j7 for every enclosing m>=1536. Read relaxed_terminal_potential_20261009_r7/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md before using any terminal strengthening. Final P_m was not refuted.

The proposed line is to retain COUPLED information from the two actual copies of H_j, with matched physical left/right query pairs and their projection normal rows. These copies see the same function and are tied by the intervening physical bridge; their starts are not independent. Cross-child rows might pay a child primitive defect that cannot be paid by throwing every child remainder away. The universal H3 U law supplies a concrete accepted base, rather than a new assumed scalar state menu.

For clarity, merely assuming every two-start H_j map is nonexpansive is forbidden. Even when the objective has a good rate, its local sensitivity can be large. Any relation between matched child paths must be derived from their actual same-D projections, their original bridge-linked starts, and all cross rows. Auxiliary samples introduce no physical step, and their values cannot be taken from independent maximizing runs.

An exact parent identity that may help is in accepted Part B Section5:

    U_M(x)-U_D(w)
     =[V_left+Theta||P(v)-P(w/D)||^2+positive physical/aux normal terms]/M
       +Rem(x,M,zP),

where v is the actual left end, w=v-(1+k)P(v), C=1+S_left, h=C+k, M=D+2h,
Theta=h[C D-k(C+D+k)]/(D+k), and zP uses actual physical gradients and P(w/D). The complete formula, signs and mixtures must be read in the accepted proof. The right-child initial auxiliary maximizer is exactly P(w/D). No reset or extra radius is permitted.

A second line, if used, must address a specific demonstrated loss with additional genuinely coupled boundary data or a different actual full certificate. It cannot be a relabeled fixed-source terminal fan, a new Green parametrization, a finite table, or another unsupported q_j profile.
